Let's formulate a combined algorithm:
Start a (sum 1). For several iterations:
  1. Compute w = LSE weights of a*a (active lags). 
  2. Update a using exponentiated gradient on a to flatten a*a: a <- softmax(log a - η * G_a), where G_a from LSE of a*a. This is local optimization.
  3. Update g to flatten a*g: Initialize g=a. For m steps, compute y = a*g, w_g = LSE weights β_g(y), update g <- softmax(log g - η_g * C_a^T w_g). Since g nonnegative sum1.
  4. Blend a <- (1-τ)a + τ g, or multiplicative a <- a^{1-τ} g^τ (geometric) to maintain positivity.
This alternation may find better equilibrium. It's like Sinkhorn? If a and g both flatten their cross conv and each self? At convergence a≈g and a*a flat.

We can implement efficient operations:
- self_conv(a) via FFT.

```
def d_and_dmax(f, S): c=fast_self_conv(f); d=n*c/(S*S); dmax=max(d)
def lse_score_p(p, beta):
   c=fast_self_conv(p); S=1
   d=n*c
   dmax=d.max()
   z=beta*(d-dmax)
   # If beta too big, z min maybe -inf; subtract max = z max 0 already
   e=exp(z)
   sum_e=e.sum()
   L=dmax + log(sum_e)/beta
   F=2*L
   w=e/sum_e
   h=corr_pair(p,w)  # q_i
   cweighted = dot(w, c)??? Need G_a=4n ∑w(q_i - c_across?) Wait earlier G_i=4n∑w(q_i - c_k)? Is c_k independent of i; yes c_k is convolution value. So G_i =4n( h_i - ∑_k w_k c_k ) (with S=1). Previous grad = 4n h -4 LDS =4n h -4n dot(w,c). Yes.
   G = 4*n*(h - cw)
   # G dot p = 4n(∑p h - cw sumw). ∑p h = ∑w c = cw. zero.
   grad_p = G # tangent
   grad_z = p * grad_p  # if p=softmax(z).
   return F, grad_other

F = 2 L. L = LSE(d). ∂L/∂p_i = ∑_k w_k ∂d_k/∂p_i. d_k = n c_k (since S=1). ∂c_k/∂p_i = 2 q_i(k) (where q_i(k)=∑_{j: i+j=k} p_j, with valid). So ∂d/∂p_i = 2n ∑ w_k q_i(k)=2n h_i. ```
def self_and_corr(p, w):
   n=len(p); M=2*n-1; L=next_power_of_two(M)
   F=np.fft.rfft(p, L)
   c=np.fft.irfft(F*F, L)[:M]
   # w length M
   Wm=np.zeros(L); Wm[:M]=w[::-1]
   FW=np.fft.rfft(Wm)
   y=np.fft.irfft(F * FW, L)  # F uses p; product F*FW corresponds conv p with Wm? In frequency, F(p)*F(Wm)=linear conv if L sufficient. Yes.
   h=y[n-1:M][::-1]
   return c,h
```

Function `active_score_tail(y, ctx)`:
```
n,M,L,active_idx,invK,FW_active,use_direct,alpha,beta_lse = ctx
z=np.empty(n); z[0]=0; z[1:]=y
zmax=z.max(); e=np.exp(z-zmax); p=e/e.sum()
Fp=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
c=np.fft.irfft(Fp*Fp,L)[:M]
F=0.0
G=np.zeros(n)
if active_idx is not None:
   cw = float(c[active_idx].mean())
   F += 2.0*n*cw
   if use_direct:
      h=active_h_direct(p, active_idx, invK)
   else:
      yarr=np.fft.irfft(Fp*FW_active,L)
      h=yarr[n-1:M][::-1]  # already weighted if FW_active built with w_act
   G += 4.0*n*(h - cw)
if alpha>0:
   d=n*c; dmax=float(d.max()); zb=beta_lse*(d-dmax); eb=exp(zb); se=eb.sum(); w=eb/se
   Lse=dmax+log(se)/beta_lse
   F += alpha*2.0*Lse
   # h_lse
   Wm=np.zeros(L); Wm[:M]=w[::-1]; FW=np.fft.rfft(Wm)
   yarr=np.fft.irfft(Fp*FW,L); hL=yarr[n-1:M][::-1]
   cwL=float(np.dot(w,c))
   G += alpha*4.0*n*(hL-cwL)
grad=p*G
# project? G sum p G maybe small. We can subtract mean to ensure tangent: grad -= np.mean(p*? Actually for log tail, we need full gradient full: p_i G_i - p_i sum(p_j G_j). G from tangent sum should be zero but numeric. Compute proj=sum(p*G); grad = p*(G - proj)
return F, grad[1:]
```

Now, coarse-to-fine optimization using active contexts. Let's design a generic `refine(p, deadline, label)` that does a sequence of active/LSE L-BFGS runs plus subgrad, tracking best.

```
def refine(p, deadline, label):
    p=clean normalize; best=p; bscore=exact(p)
    n=len(p)
    # schedule list of (K, alpha, beta, time_frac)
    sched = [
      (None? pure lse?), maybe (32,1.0,500,0.15),
      (64,0.5,1500,0.20),
      (128,0.2,5000,0.25),
      (256,0.1,15000,0.25),
      (512,0.05,50000,0.15)
    ]
    total_time = deadline - now
    for K,alpha,beta,frac in sched:
       sub_dead = now + frac*total_remaining? Use cumulative from initial total.
       while time < sub_dead:
          ctx = make_context(p, K, alpha, beta) # active set based on current p before each run
          mf=estimate
          sc,p2=run_context(p, ctx, sub_dead, mf)
          if sc<best: best=p2
          p=normalize(p2) maybe if exact improved or LSE final? We should update p to p2 if exact not worse? Could be LSE improved but exact worse; for next active, use p2? Could choose p2 if exact <= best*1.05? Maybe use p2 if finite. To avoid regress, keep `p = p2` if exact(p2) <= exact(p)+small, else p=best? Since active set based on current peaks; if LSE run increased exact, maybe still good shape. We can use `p = normalize(p2)` as long exact < 2.5. But final best tracked.
       if subgrad time >30: subgrad_polish with deadline min(sub_dead-10, deadline-...); update best; p=best or polished.
    return best,bscore
```
But if total_time allocated small, schedule skip. For main trajectory, use deadline.

Let's decide static:
```
def estimate_maxfun(rem, n):
    if rem < 4: return 0
    L=next_power_of_two(2*n-1)
    scale = (L/32768.0) * math.sqrt(max(1.0, n/10240.0))
    est = 0.012 * scale
    mf = int(0.45 * rem / max(est,1e-4))
    mf = max(80, min(30000, mf))
    return mf
```
For n=512, L=1024, scale=0.031*0.223=0.007, est=0.00008, rem10 ->0.45*10/8e-5 huge cap 30000, but small eval 10k/ ? okay. Use min per maybe. For small n, L-BFGS fast; cap 30000 maybe too many but time small. Could cap 5000 for n<2048. Add `if n<2048: cap=5000`. Good.

def run_lbfgs_context(p, ctx, deadline, maxfun):

   # ensure p[0] not zero

   bounds=[(-500,500)]*(n-1)
   opts=...
   try res=minimize(active_score_tail,y0,jac=True,args=(ctx,),bounds=bounds,options=opts)
   except: return exact_score(p),p
   if res.x: reconstruct p2: z=empty; z[0]=0; z[1:]=res.x; p2=softmax

Now `subgrad_polish` details. Need use exact_score many times. We can implement local exact with `fast_self_conv`. Use `np.argpartition`.
```
def subgrad_polish(p, deadline, max_iters=8):
   p=normalize(clean); best=p; bsc=exact
   for it in range(max_iters):
      if time > deadline: break
      c=fast_self_conv(p); n=len(p); d=n*c; dmax=float(d.max()); M=2*n-1
      if dmax<=0: break
      # top all for up to 12
      topK=12 if M>=12 else M
      order=np.argpartition(d, M-topK)[-topK:]
      order=order[np.argsort(d[order])[::-1]]
      improved=False
      # LSE modes
      for beta in (2000, 8000, 30000):
         zb=beta*(d-dmax); eb=exp; se; w
         FW=make_w_fft(w,L,n,M) # need L
         # Need Fp? h = ? We don't have Fp. Use fast_hankel(p,w,L)
         h=fast_hankel(p,w,L)
         cw=dot(w,c); G=h-cw
         improved |= try_direction(p,best,bsc,G,deadline)
      # top k
      for k in (1,2,3,4,6,8,12): if k<=topK:
         idx=order[:k]; cw=c[idx].mean(); h=active_h_direct(p,idx,1/k); G=h-cw
         improved |= try_direction...
      if not improved: break
   return bsc,best
```
Need `fast_hankel(p,w,L)` distinct from active precomputed. Implement:
```
def fast_hankel(p,w,L):
   n=len(p); M=2*n-1; Fp=np.fft.rfft(p,L); Wm=zeros(L); Wm[:M]=w[::-1]; FW=rfft(Wm); y=irfft(Fp*FW,L); return y[n-1:M][::-1]
```
This computes Fp again; in subgrad not a big deal. In LSE modes, topK order uses d.
`try_direction` top-level:
```
def try_direction(p, current_score, G, step_list):
   logp=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300))
   s=float(np.max(np.abs(G)))
   if s<1e-20: return current_score
   Gn=G/s
   for sigma in (0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.8,1.5):
       logq=logp - sigma*Gn
       q=exp(logq-max); q/=sum; sc=exact_score(q); if sc<current: current=sc; p=q
   return current
```
But this returns updated p? Need return p too. We'll have `try_direction` return (score,p). Top-level.
Step list maybe too large; if G direction bad, some overshoots. Accept best.
Need if deadline near, skip.

Could use precomputed `logp` each direction; recomputing maybe. Fine.

Now `perturb_sequence(p, rng)`:
- modes: add smooth noise to log, blend with uniform, reverse with noise, endpoint boost. Return normalize. Use low-pass noise as previous. Ensure positive.
```
def perturb_sequence(p,rng):
  p=normalize(clean); n=len(p); mode=rng.integers(0,5)
  if mode0: logp=log(p+1e-300)+0.2*rng.standard_normal(n); lowpass? maybe
  mode1: t=0.3 blend uniform
  mode2: q=p[::-1]*(1+0.1normal)
  mode3: endpoint boost: x=linspace; q=p*(1+0.2*lognormal endpoints)
  mode4: random smooth noise via FFT low-pass with p * exp(noise)
  return normalize(clip)

Let's implement weighted direct:
```
def weighted_direct_h(p, idx, weights, inv_sum=1.0):
   n=len(p); h=zeros
   for k,wk in zip(idx, weights):
      if abs(wk)<1e-15: continue
      if k<n: seg=p[:k+1][::-1]
      else: lo=k-n+1; seg=p[lo:n][::-1]
      h[segment] += wk*seg
   h *= inv_sum
   return h
```
For uniform K, weights=1/K, inv_sum=1. Or weights=1, inv_sum=1/K. We'll use weights actual and inv_sum=1 to avoid. In ctx, store `weights` for sparse. Need direct loop with lengths; for each k, segment length up to n, multiplying by scalar. Good.

Now, code specifics. Let's write functions carefully.

`fast_self_conv`: 
```
def fast_self_conv(p):
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n=int(p.size)
    if n==0: return np.empty(0)
    M=2*n-1; L=next_power_of_two(M)
    F=np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(F*F, L)[:M]
    # remove tiny imaginary? irfft real
    c=np.nan_to_num(c, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    return c
```
For exact_score, p normalized sum1. If p very small? okay.

`h_from_fft`:
```
def h_from_fft(Fp, FW, L, n, M):
    y=np.fft.irfft(Fp*FW, L)
    seg=y[n-1:M]
    return seg[::-1]
```
Need ensure Fp and FW same shape. FW built rfft with same L.

`make_w_fft(w, L, M)`:
```
def make_w_fft(w, L, M):
   Wm=np.zeros(L)
   Wm[:M]=w[::-1]
   return np.fft.rfft(Wm, L)
```
If w length maybe M. Good.

`weighted_direct_h` with segment assignment. Use for (idx, weights):
```
def weighted_direct_h(p, idx, weights, inv=1.0):
    n=len(p); h=np.zeros(n)
    for kk, wk in zip(idx, weights):
       kk=int(kk); wk=float(wk)
       if abs(wk) < 1e-15: continue
       if kk < n:
          length=kk+1
          h[:length] += wk * p[:length][::-1]
       else:
          lo=kk-n+1
          length=n-lo
          h[lo:n] += wk * p[lo:n][::-1]
    if inv != 1.0: h *= inv
    return h
```
For `weights` sum1, inv=1.

`make_weight_ctx(w, n, alpha=0.0, beta_lse=0.0)`:
```
def make_weight_ctx(w, n, alpha, beta_lse):
    M=2*n-1; L=next_power_of_two(M)
    w=np.asarray(w,float).ravel()
    if w.size != M: # pad? no
    w=np.nan_to_num; w=np.clip(w,0,None); s=w.sum(); if s<1e-15: return make_lse_ctx(n, max(1,beta_lse), )? If alpha? 
    w=w/s
    cnt=int(np.count_nonzero(w > 1e-12))
    if cnt <= DIRECT_WEIGHT_LIMIT:
       idx=np.nonzero(w)[0].astype(np.int64)
       wts=w[idx]
       ctx=(n,M,L,None,None,idx,wts,True, float(alpha), float(beta_lse if alpha>0 else 0.0))
    else:
       FW=make_w_fft(w,L,M)
       ctx=(n,M,L,w,FW,None,None,False, float(alpha), float(beta_lse if alpha>0 else 0.0))
    return ctx
```
If alpha=0, beta 0. If pure fixed and cnt small, w_dense None; objective uses dot(sparse_weights,c[idx]).

`make_lse_ctx(n,beta)`: M,L; ctx=(n,M,L,None,None,None,None,False,1.0, max(1.0,float(beta))).

def refine_sequence(p0, deadline, label):
    p=normalize_sum(p0)
    if p.size>1 and p[-1] > p[0]: p=p[::-1]
    best=p.copy(); bsc=exact_score(p)
    print label start
    t_start=time.monotonic(); avail=deadline - t_start
    if avail < 30:
       sc,best2=subgrad_polish(p, deadline, max_iters=2); if sc<bsc: ...; return bsc,best
    if avail < 120: sched short
    else sched full
    cum=0.0
    for stage in sched:
       cum += frac
       sub_dead=t_start + cum*avail; if sub_dead>deadline: sub_dead=deadline
       if time >= sub_dead-10: continue
       runs=0
       while time < sub_dead - 15 and runs<3:
          ctx=build_context(p, mode,param,alpha,beta)
          mf=estimate_maxfun(sub_dead - time,n)
          if mf<50: break
          sc,p2=run_lbfgs_context(p,ctx,sub_dead,mf)
          cur_sc=sc
          if np.isfinite(cur_sc):
             if cur_sc < bsc: best=p2.copy(); bsc=cur_sc; print
             # update p
             # if p2 not terrible
             raw_sc=exact_score(p)? We need current p exact. Keep cur_exact variable.
          runs+=1
       # subgrad after stage if time left
       if time < sub_dead - 25:
           sc2,p2=subgrad_polish(p, min(sub_dead-10, deadline), max_iters=3)
           if sc2<bsc: best=p2; bsc=sc2
           if sc2<=exact_score(p)+0.01: p=p2
    # end
    # final quick high pure lse if time left before deadline
    if time < deadline - 20:
       ctx=build_context(p,0,30000,0,30000)? maybe one run
    return bsc,best
```

Pseudo code:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+float(budget_s)-30
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    parent=None
    try:

def estimate_maxfun(rem,n):
    if rem <= 0: return 0
    L=next_power_of_two(2*n-1)
    scale=(L/32768.0)*math.sqrt(max(1.0,n/10240.0))
    est=0.010*scale
    mf=int(0.55*rem/max(est,1e-6))
    if n < 2048: cap=4000
    elif n < 8192: cap=12000
    else: cap=30000
    return max(60, min(cap, mf))
```

Implementation of `add_init`:
```
def add_init(inits, cap, arr):
    if len(inits) >= cap:
        return
    a = clean_arr(arr)
    if a.size == 0:
        return
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    if float(a.sum()) < 1e-15:
        a = np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s > 0:
        a = a / s
    inits.append(a.astype(np.float64, copy=False))
```
If arr has negatives, clip. Good.

```
def perturb_sequence(p, rng):
   p=normalize_sum(p); n=p.size; if n==0 return p
   mode=int(rng.integers(0,5))
   if mode==0:
      noise=rng.standard_normal(n)
      if n>16:
         L=next_power_of_two(n)
         freqs=np.fft.rfftfreq(L)*L; power=1/((np.abs(freqs)+1)**2.0); phase=rng.uniform(-np.pi,np.pi,len(power)); spec=power*np.exp(1j*phase); spec[0]=0; smooth=np.fft.irfft(spec,L)[:n]
      else: smooth=noise
      q=np.exp(np.log(p+1e-300)+0.2*smooth)
   elif mode==1: t=0.3; q=(1-t)*p+t*(np.ones(n)/n)
   elif mode==2: q=p[::-1]*(1+0.05*rng.standard_normal(n))
   elif mode==3:
      x=np.linspace(0,1,n); boost=1+0.3*np.exp(-((x-0.5)**2)/0.02)? Actually endpoint boost: 1+0.3*(np.exp(-x/0.1)+np.exp(-(1-x)/0.1))
      q=p*boost
   else:
      q=p* (0.8+0.4*rng.random(n))
   return normalize_sum(np.clip(q,0,None))
```
No nested.

def clean_arr(x):

    except Exception:

    if a.size==0: return a

`refine_sequence` schedule tuples: ensure 5 elements. Full schedule maybe 7; sum fractions: 0.10+0.15+0.18+0.17+0.12+0.15+0.13=1.0. Short: 0.5+0.5.
In refine loop, need `mode,param,alpha,beta,frac = stage`. Then after run, stage_mode=mode.

`refine_sequence` if `p_score` variable. After subgrad, if `sc2 < bsc` update. If `sc2 <= p_score + 1e-8` maybe set p? Use `if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 <= p_score + 1e-8: p=p2; p_score=sc2`? But if sc2 slightly worse, keep p. Implement:
```

    if sc2 < bsc: bsc=sc2; best=p2.copy()
    if sc2 <= p_score + 1e-9:
        p=p2; p_score=sc2
```
But if p2 same. Fine.

At end of refine, maybe set p=best? Not needed. Return best.

`propose_candidate` after refine start: update if `sc < best_score`. But refine_sequence returns best within start, which might be lower than global. Good.

Final quick fixed context: If best_seq maybe length small. Use `make_fixed_lse_weight_ctx(best_seq, 20000, 0.0, 0.0)` and run_lbfgs_context. But if best_seq endpoint low, run reverses. Good. However running `run_lbfgs_context` with alpha=0 pure fixed high weights may slightly increase exact; update if lower.

Perturb restarts: `refine_sequence` full schedule may be too long for small left; deadline sub_dead. Good.

Need ensure final output length not too large? If best_seq length > 20000 maybe resample to 16384? Our lengths <=16384. Good.

Let's consider if coordinate count primary 16384 and L-BFGS tail 16383; bounds list memory ~16k tuples, okay.

One more mathematical improvement: Use **symmetrization** after final? Since objective invariant under reversal, maybe symmetrizing best with reversed could lower due average? Could try: `q=normalize(0.5*(p+p[::-1]))`, exact score maybe. We can evaluate and if better, use. Add final step. The convolution of symmetrized is average? Not guaranteed but often. Evaluate cheap. Double-check direct H formula with weighted nonuniform for k< n: For k=1,n=2: h0 += w1*p0? Wait formula `h[:k+1] += wk * p[:k+1][::-1]`. For k=1: h[0]+=w*p1, h[1]+=w*p0. Earlier expected h0=w1*p1? For derivative wrt p0 for lag1: ∂c1/∂p0 = 2w1 p1. Yes. h1=w p0. Good. For k=0: h0+=w0*p0. Good.
For k>=n: n=5,k=5: lo=1,h[1:5]+=wk*p[1:5][::-1] => h1+=p4, h2+=p3,h3+=p2,h4+=p1. Expected i=1 j=4, yes. Good. k=8,n=5: lo=4,h4+=p4. Good.

Self conv c via FFT zero-padded length L>=M. Good.

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative sequence minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)^2
using:
  - FFT-based self-convolution and Hankel-gradient products
  - log-parameterized L-BFGS-B on the simplex
  - adaptive log-sum-exp objectives
  - fixed active-set / LSE-weight quadratic subproblems
  - multiplicative subgradient polishing
  - multi-scale starts and coarse-to-fine refinement
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.optimize import minimize

DIRECT_WEIGHT_LIMIT = 12


def next_power_of_two(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def first_key(item):
    return item[0]


def clean_arr(x):
    try:
        a = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        a = np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    return a


def normalize_sum(arr):
    a = clean_arr(arr)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-15:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    return (a / s).astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def resample_sequence(a, n):
    a = clean_arr(a)
    n = int(max(1, n))
    m = int(a.size)
    if m == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    if m == 1:
        return np.full(n, float(a[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    if m == n:
        return a.astype(np.float64, copy=False)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a).astype(np.float64)
    out = np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    return np.clip(out, 0.0, None)


def fast_self_conv(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(p.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    M = 2 * n - 1
    L = next_power_of_two(M)
    F = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(F * F, L)[:M]
    c = np.nan_to_num(c, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return np.maximum(c, 0.0).astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def exact_score(p):
    a = clean_arr(p)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-15:
        return np.inf
    a = a / s
    c = fast_self_conv(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if cmax < 0.0:
        cmax = 0.0
    val = 2.0 * float(n) * cmax
    return val if np.isfinite(val) else np.inf


def make_w_fft(w, L, M):
    Wm = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    Wm[:M] = w[::-1]
    return np.fft.rfft(Wm, L)


def h_from_fft(Fp, FW, L, n, M):
    y = np.fft.irfft(Fp * FW, L)
    y = y[n - 1:M]
    return y[::-1]


def weighted_direct_h(p, idx, weights, inv=1.0):
    n = int(p.size)
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for kk, wk in zip(idx, weights):
        kk = int(kk)
        wk = float(wk)
        if abs(wk) < 1e-15:
            continue
        if kk < n:
            length = kk + 1
            h[:length] += wk * p[:length][::-1]
        else:
            lo = kk - n + 1
            if lo < 0:
                lo = 0
            length = n - lo
            h[lo:n] += wk * p[lo:n][::-1]
    if inv != 1.0:
        h *= float(inv)
    return h


def make_lse_ctx(n, beta):
    n = int(n)
    M = 2 * n - 1
    L = next_power_of_two(M)
    beta = float(beta)
    if not np.isfinite(beta) or beta <= 0.0:
        beta = 50.0
    return (n, M, L, None, None, None, None, False, 1.0, beta)


def make_weight_ctx(w, n, alpha, beta_lse):
    n = int(n)
    M = 2 * n - 1
    L = next_power_of_two(M)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if w.size != M:
        return make_lse_ctx(n, max(1.0, float(beta_lse)))
    w = np.nan_to_num(w, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    w = np.clip(w, 0.0, None)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s < 1e-15:
        return make_lse_ctx(n, max(1.0, float(beta_lse)))
    w = w / s
    nonzero = w > 1e-12
    cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(nonzero))
    alpha = float(alpha) if np.isfinite(alpha) else 0.0
    beta = float(beta_lse) if np.isfinite(beta_lse) else 0.0
    if alpha <= 0.0:
        beta = 0.0

    if cnt <= DIRECT_WEIGHT_LIMIT and cnt > 0:
        idx = np.nonzero(nonzero)[0].astype(np.int64)
        wts = w[idx].astype(np.float64, copy=False)
        return (n, M, L, None, None, idx, wts, True, alpha, beta)
    FW = make_w_fft(w, L, M)
    return (n, M, L, w, FW, None, None, False, alpha, beta)


def make_top_ctx(p, K, alpha, beta_lse):
    p = normalize_sum(p)
    n = int(p.size)
    M = 2 * n - 1
    c = fast_self_conv(p)
    d = float(n) * c
    K = int(max(1, K))
    K = int(min(K, max(1, M // 4)))
    if K >= M:
        idx = np.arange(M, dtype=np.int64)
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(d, M - K)[-K:].astype(np.int64)
    w = np.zeros(M, dtype=np.float64)
    w[idx] = 1.0 / float(K)
    return make_weight_ctx(w, n, alpha, beta_lse)


def make_fixed_lse_weight_ctx(p, beta_w, alpha, beta_lse):
    p = normalize_sum(p)
    n = int(p.size)
    M = 2 * n - 1
    c = fast_self_conv(p)
    d = float(n) * c
    dmax = float(np.max(d))
    beta_w = float(max(1.0, beta_w))
    z = beta_w * (d - dmax)
    e = np.exp(z)
    se = float(e.sum())
    if se <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(se):
        w = np.ones(M, dtype=np.float64) / float(M)
    else:
        w = e / se
    return make_weight_ctx(w, n, alpha, beta_lse)


def build_context(p, mode, param, alpha, beta_lse):
    n = int(len(p))
    mode = int(mode)
    if mode == 0:
        beta = float(param) if np.isfinite(param) and float(param) > 0.0 else 50.0
        return make_lse_ctx(n, beta)
    if mode == 1:
        beta_w = float(param) if np.isfinite(param) and float(param) > 0.0 else 1000.0
        return make_fixed_lse_weight_ctx(p, beta_w, alpha, beta_lse)
    if mode == 2:
        K = int(param) if np.isfinite(param) else 32
        return make_top_ctx(p, K, alpha, beta_lse)
    beta = float(beta_lse) if np.isfinite(beta_lse) and float(beta_lse) > 0.0 else 100.0
    return make_lse_ctx(n, beta)


def softmax_from_tail(y):
    n = int(len(y)) + 1
    z = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    z[0] = 0.0
    z[1:] = y
    z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=-100.0, posinf=500.0, neginf=-500.0)
    z = np.clip(z, -500.0, 500.0)
    m = float(z.max())
    e = np.exp(z - m)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def active_score_tail(y, ctx):
    n, M, L, w_dense, FW_dense, sparse_idx, sparse_wts, use_direct, alpha, beta_lse = ctx
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1:
        return 0.0, []

    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if y.size != n - 1:
        return np.inf, np.zeros(max(0, n - 1), dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(y)):
        return np.inf, np.zeros(max(0, n - 1), dtype=np.float64)

    p = softmax_from_tail(y)
    Fp = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(Fp * Fp, L)[:M]
    c = np.nan_to_num(c, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)

    Fval = 0.0
    G = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    if use_direct:
        if sparse_idx is None or sparse_wts is None:
            pass
        else:
            cw = float(np.dot(sparse_wts, c[sparse_idx]))
            h = weighted_direct_h(p, sparse_idx, sparse_wts)
            Fval += 2.0 * float(n) * cw
            G += 4.0 * float(n) * (h - cw)
    elif FW_dense is not None:
        if w_dense is None:
            cw = 0.0
            h = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            cw = float(np.dot(w_dense, c))
            h = h_from_fft(Fp, FW_dense, L, n, M)
        Fval += 2.0 * float(n) * cw
        G += 4.0 * float(n) * (h - cw)

    if alpha > 0.0:
        beta_lse = float(beta_lse)
        if not np.isfinite(beta_lse) or beta_lse <= 0.0:
            beta_lse = 50.0
        d = float(n) * c
        dmax = float(np.max(d))
        if not np.isfinite(dmax) or dmax <= 0.0:
            dmax = 0.0
        z = beta_lse * (d - dmax)
        e = np.exp(z)
        se = float(e.sum())
        if se <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(se):
            se = 1.0
            w = np.ones(M, dtype=np.float64) / float(M)
            lse = dmax
        else:
            w = e / se
            lse = dmax + math.log(se) / beta_lse
        Fval += alpha * 2.0 * lse
        FW_lse = make_w_fft(w, L, M)
        h_lse = h_from_fft(Fp, FW_lse, L, n, M)
        cw_lse = float(np.dot(w, c))
        G += alpha * 4.0 * float(n) * (h_lse - cw_lse)

    if not np.isfinite(Fval):
        return np.inf, np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)

    proj = float(np.dot(p, G))
    grad_full = p * (G - proj)
    grad_full = np.nan_to_num(grad_full, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    grad_full = np.clip(grad_full, -1e8, 1e8)
    return float(Fval), grad_full[1:].astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def estimate_maxfun(rem, n):
    rem = float(max(0.0, rem))
    if rem < 3.0:
        return 0
    n = int(n)
    L = next_power_of_two(2 * n - 1)
    scale = (float(L) / 32768.0) * math.sqrt(max(1.0, float(n) / 10240.0))
    est = 0.018 * scale
    mf = int(0.45 * rem / max(est, 1e-6))
    if n < 2048:
        cap = 4000
    elif n < 8192:
        cap = 12000
    else:
        cap = 30000
    mf = max(60, min(cap, mf))
    return mf


def run_lbfgs_context(p, ctx, deadline, maxfun):
    p = normalize_sum(p)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf, p
    if n > 1 and float(p[-1]) > float(p[0]) * 1.2:
        p = p[::-1]
        p = normalize_sum(p)

    sc0 = exact_score(p)
    if n <= 1 or not np.isfinite(sc0):
        return sc0, p

    rem = float(deadline - time.monotonic())
    if rem < 4.0:
        return sc0, p

    pb = max(float(p[0]), 1e-300)
    y0 = np.log(np.clip(p, 1e-300, None) / pb)
    y0 = y0[1:]
    y0 = np.nan_to_num(y0, nan=-80.0, posinf=500.0, neginf=-500.0)
    y0 = np.clip(y0, -500.0, 500.0)

    mf = estimate_maxfun(rem * 0.75, n)
    if mf is None or mf < 60:
        if maxfun is not None:
            mf = int(min(int(maxfun), mf if mf else 60))
        else:
            mf = 60
    if maxfun is not None:
        mf = int(min(mf, int(maxfun)))
    if mf < 60:
        return sc0, p

    bounds = [(-500.0, 500.0)] * (n - 1)
    opts = {
        "ftol": 1e-15,
        "gtol": 1e-10,
        "maxls": 30,
        "maxcor": 20,
        "disp": 0,
        "maxiter": int(max(60, mf)),
        "maxfun": int(mf),
    }

    try:
        res = minimize(active_score_tail, y0, jac=True, args=(ctx,),
                       method="L-BFGS-B", bounds=bounds, options=opts)
        x = getattr(res, "x", None)
        if x is None:
            p2 = p
        else:
            p2 = softmax_from_tail(np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64))
            if p2.size > 1 and float(p2[-1]) > float(p2[0]) * 1.2:
                p2 = p2[::-1]
            p2 = normalize_sum(p2)
    except Exception:
        p2 = p

    sc2 = exact_score(p2)
    if not np.isfinite(sc2):
        return sc0, p
    return float(sc2), p2


def add_init(inits, cap, arr):
    if len(inits) >= cap:
        return
    a = clean_arr(arr)
    if a.size == 0:
        return
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-15:
        a = np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        a = a / s
    inits.append(a.astype(np.float64, copy=False))


def generate_inits(n, rng, parent, cap=20):
    n = int(max(2, n))
    inits = []

    if parent is not None and parent.size > 0:
        pr = resample_sequence(parent, n)
        add_init(inits, cap, pr)
        add_init(inits, cap, pr[::-1])

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    u = np.abs(2.0 * x - 1.0)

    add_init(inits, cap, np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    for pp in (0.5, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 4.0):
        for alpha in (0.25, 0.6, 1.2, 2.0, 3.0):
            add_init(inits, cap, 1.0 + alpha * np.power(u, pp))

    for sigma in (0.05, 0.08, 0.12, 0.20, 0.35):
        for alpha in (0.5, 2.0, 6.0):
            g = np.exp(-(x / sigma) ** 2) + np.exp(-((1.0 - x) / sigma) ** 2)
            add_init(inits, cap, 1.0 + alpha * g)

    for alpha in (0.25, 0.55, 0.85, 0.98):
        add_init(inits, cap, 1.0 + alpha * np.cos(np.pi * x))

    for alpha in (0.5, 1.5, 3.0):
        add_init(inits, cap, 1.0 + alpha * (x ** 2 + (1.0 - x) ** 2))

    for _ in range(6):
        L = next_power_of_two(max(16, n))
        freqs = np.fft.rfftfreq(L) * L
        gamma = float(rng.uniform(1.2, 4.0))
        power = 1.0 / ((np.abs(freqs) + 1.0) ** gamma)
        power[0] = 0.0
        phase = rng.uniform(-np.pi, np.pi, size=len(power))
        spec = power * np.exp(1j * phase)
        spec[0] = 0.0
        noise = np.fft.irfft(spec, L)[:n]
        noise = noise - float(np.mean(noise))
        base = 1.0 + 0.5 * u
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.25, 0.9))
        add_init(inits, cap, base + amp * noise)

    for _ in range(5):
        vals = 1.0 + 0.4 * u
        K = int(rng.integers(2, 6))
        for _ in range(K):
            f = int(rng.integers(1, max(2, n // 12)))
            amp = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.35))
            ph = float(rng.uniform(-np.pi, np.pi))
            vals += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * x + ph)
        add_init(inits, cap, vals)

    for _ in range(4):
        a = float(rng.uniform(-2.5, 2.5))
        b = float(rng.uniform(-3.0, 3.0))
        vals = np.exp(a * x + b * (x - 0.5) ** 2)
        add_init(inits, cap, vals + 0.1 + u)

    for _ in range(3):
        vals = 0.05 + 0.95 * rng.random(n)
        vals *= (1.0 + 0.8 * u)
        add_init(inits, cap, vals)

    return inits


def perturb_sequence(p, rng):
    p = normalize_sum(p)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n == 0:
        return p
    mode = int(rng.integers(0, 5))

    if mode == 0:
        if n > 16:
            L = next_power_of_two(n)
            freqs = np.fft.rfftfreq(L) * L
            power = 1.0 / ((np.abs(freqs) + 1.0) ** 2.0)
            power[0] = 0.0
            phase = rng.uniform(-np.pi, np.pi, size=len(power))
            spec = power * np.exp(1j * phase)
            spec[0] = 0.0
            smooth = np.fft.irfft(spec, L)[:n]
        else:
            smooth = rng.standard_normal(n)
        smooth = smooth - float(smooth.mean())
        q = np.exp(np.log(p + 1e-300) + 0.18 * smooth)
    elif mode == 1:
        t = float(rng.uniform(0.15, 0.35))
        q = (1.0 - t) * p + t * (np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n))
    elif mode == 2:
        q = p[::-1] * (1.0 + 0.05 * rng.standard_normal(n))
    elif mode == 3:
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        sigma = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.18))
        boost = 1.0 + 0.5 * (np.exp(-(x / sigma) ** 2) + np.exp(-((1.0 - x) / sigma) ** 2))
        q = p * boost
    else:
        q = p * (0.75 + 0.5 * rng.random(n))

    q = np.clip(q, 0.0, None)
    return normalize_sum(q)


def try_direction(p, current_score, G):
    n = int(p.size)
    if n == 0:
        return current_score, p
    s = float(np.max(np.abs(G)))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-20:
        return current_score, p
    Gn = G / s
    logp = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    best_p = p
    best_sc = current_score
    for sigma in (0.02, 0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.4, 0.8, 1.6):
        logq = logp - sigma * Gn
        m = float(np.max(logq))
        q = np.exp(logq - m)
        qs = float(q.sum())
        if qs <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(qs):
            continue
        q = q / qs
        sc = exact_score(q)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_p = q
    return float(best_sc), best_p


def subgrad_polish(p, deadline, max_iters=8):
    p = normalize_sum(p)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf, p
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score(p)

    for _ in range(int(max_iters)):
        if time.monotonic() > float(deadline) - 1.0:
            break
        c = fast_self_conv(p)
        d = float(n) * c
        M = int(d.size)
        if M == 0:
            break
        dmax = float(np.max(d))
        if not np.isfinite(dmax) or dmax <= 0.0:
            break

        topK = min(12, M)
        if topK >= M:
            order = np.arange(M, dtype=np.int64)
        else:
            order = np.argpartition(d, M - topK)[-topK:].astype(np.int64)
            order = order[np.argsort(d[order])[::-1]]

        improved = False

        for beta in (2000, 8000, 30000):
            if time.monotonic() > float(deadline) - 1.0:
                break
            z = beta * (d - dmax)
            e = np.exp(z)
            se = float(e.sum())
            if se <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(se):
                w = np.ones(M, dtype=np.float64) / float(M)
            else:
                w = e / se
            L = next_power_of_two(M)
            h = h_from_fft(np.fft.rfft(p, L), make_w_fft(w, L, M), L, n, M)
            cw = float(np.dot(w, c))
            cur_sc, best_p = try_direction(best_p, best_sc, h - cw)
            if cur_sc < best_sc:
                improved = True
                best_sc = cur_sc

        for k in (1, 2, 3, 4, 6, 8, 12):
            if time.monotonic() > float(deadline) - 1.0:
                break
            k = min(k, topK)
            idx = order[:k]
            wts = np.full(k, 1.0 / float(k), dtype=np.float64)
            h = weighted_direct_h(best_p, idx, wts)
            cw = float(np.dot(wts, c[idx]))
            cur_sc, best_p = try_direction(best_p, best_sc, h - cw)
            if cur_sc < best_sc:
                improved = True
                best_sc = cur_sc

        if not improved:
            break

    best_sc = exact_score(best_p)
    if not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        return np.inf, p
    return float(best_sc), best_p


def refine_sequence(p0, deadline, label):
    p = normalize_sum(p0)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf, p
    if n > 1 and p[-1] > p[0]:
        p = p[::-1]
        p = normalize_sum(p)

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score(p)
    p_score = float(best_sc)
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    avail = float(deadline - t_start)
    print("[{}] start {:.8f} n={}".format(label, p_score, n), flush=True)

    if avail < 30.0:
        sc, sp = subgrad_polish(p, deadline, max_iters=2)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_p = sp
        return best_sc, best_p

    if avail < 120.0:
        sched = [
            (1, 2000, 0.2, 2000, 0.55),
            (2, 32, 0.0, 20000, 0.45),
        ]
    else:
        sched = [
            (0, 50, 0.0, 50, 0.10),
            (1, 500, 0.5, 500, 0.15),
            (1, 5000, 0.3, 5000, 0.18),
            (2, 32, 0.1, 10000, 0.17),
            (1, 30000, 0.0, 30000, 0.12),
            (2, 64, 0.0, 100000, 0.15),
            (2, 128, 0.0, 100000, 0.13),
        ]

    cum = 0.0
    stage_no = 0
    for mode, param, alpha, beta, frac in sched:
        cum += float(frac)
        if cum > 1.0:
            cum = 1.0
        sub_dead = t_start + cum * avail
        sub_dead = min(float(sub_dead), float(deadline))
        stage_no += 1
        if time.monotonic() > sub_dead - 15.0:
            continue

        runs = 0
        while time.monotonic() < sub_dead - 15.0 and runs < 3:
            maxfun = estimate_maxfun(sub_dead - time.monotonic(), n)
            if maxfun < 60:
                break
            ctx = build_context(p, mode, param, alpha, beta)
            sc, p2 = run_lbfgs_context(p, ctx, sub_dead, maxfun)
            runs += 1
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                if sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = float(sc)
                    best_p = p2.copy()
                    print("[{}] new best {:.8f} stage={} n={}".format(label, best_sc, stage_no, n), flush=True)

                tol = 0.04 if mode == 0 else 0.003
                if sc <= p_score + tol:
                    p = normalize_sum(p2)
                    if p.size > 1 and p[-1] > p[0]:
                        p = p[::-1]
                        p = normalize_sum(p)
                    p_score = float(sc)

        if time.monotonic() < sub_dead - 25.0:
            sc2, p2 = subgrad_polish(p, min(sub_dead - 5.0, deadline), max_iters=2)
            if np.isfinite(sc2):
                if sc2 < best_sc:
                    best_sc = float(sc2)
                    best_p = p2.copy()
                    print("[{}] subgrad best {:.8f}".format(label, best_sc), flush=True)
                if sc2 <= p_score + 1e-10:
                    p = normalize_sum(p2)
                    if p.size > 1 and p[-1] > p[0]:
                        p = p[::-1]
                        p = normalize_sum(p)
                    p_score = float(sc2)

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 20.0:
        ctx = make_fixed_lse_weight_ctx(p, 30000.0, 0.0, 0.0)
        sc, p2 = run_lbfgs_context(p, ctx, deadline - 5.0, 2500)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc)
            best_p = p2.copy()
            print("[{}] final fixed-LSE best {:.8f}".format(label, best_sc), flush=True)

    return float(best_sc), best_p


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = float(max(60.0, budget_s))
    deadline = t0 + total_budget - 30.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    parent = None
    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if h is not None:
            arr = clean_arr(h)
            if arr.size >= 32:
                parent = normalize_sum(arr)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        if parent.size > 16384:
            parent = resample_sequence(parent, 16384)
            parent = normalize_sum(parent)
        parent_len = int(parent.size)
    else:
        parent_len = 10240

    if parent_len < 2048:
        primary_len = 2048
    elif parent_len > 16384:
        primary_len = 16384
    else:
        primary_len = parent_len

    length_candidates = [
        primary_len,
        min(16384, max(512, 2 * primary_len)),
        max(512, primary_len // 2),
        4096,
        1024,
        512,
    ]
    lengths = []
    for L in length_candidates:
        if 512 <= L <= 16384:
            duplicate = False
            for e in lengths:
                if int(e) == int(L):
                    duplicate = True
                    break
            if not duplicate:
                lengths.append(int(L))
            if len(lengths) >= 4:
                break

    print("Parent length: {}".format(parent_len if parent is not None else None), flush=True)
    print("Primary length: {} lengths: {}".format(primary_len, lengths), flush=True)

    init_pool = []
    for n in lengths:
        cap = 14 if n >= 12000 else (20 if n >= 4096 else 12)
        inits = generate_inits(int(n), rng, parent, cap=cap)
        for arr in inits:
            p = normalize_sum(arr)
            sc = exact_score(p)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                init_pool.append((float(sc), p, int(n)))

    if not init_pool:
        p = np.ones(primary_len, dtype=np.float64) / float(primary_len)
        init_pool.append((2.0, p, int(primary_len)))

    init_pool.sort(key=first_key)

    best_seq = init_pool[0][1].copy()
    best_score = init_pool[0][0]
    print("Best initial: {:.8f} length={}".format(best_score, best_seq.size), flush=True)

    starts = []
    seen_shapes = []

    def add_start(q):
        q = normalize_sum(q)
        if q.size > 1 and q[-1] > q[0]:
            q = q[::-1]
            q = normalize_sum(q)
        sc = exact_score(q)
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            return
        for r in seen_shapes:
            if r.size == q.size and float(np.linalg.norm(r - q)) < 1e-7:
                return
        seen_shapes.append(q.copy())
        starts.append((float(sc), q))

    if parent is not None:
        add_start(resample_sequence(parent, primary_len))
    else:
        for sc, p, n in init_pool:
            if int(n) == int(primary_len):
                add_start(p)
                break

    for sc, p, n in init_pool:
        if len(starts) >= 3:
            break
        if int(n) == int(primary_len):
            add_start(p)

    for n in lengths:
        if len(starts) >= 4:
            break
        if int(n) == int(primary_len):
            continue
        best_for_n = None
        best_for_sc = np.inf
        for sc, p, nn in init_pool:
            if int(nn) == int(n) and sc < best_for_sc:
                best_for_sc = sc
                best_for_n = p
        if best_for_n is not None:
            add_start(best_for_n)

    while len(starts) < 3:
        add_start(perturb_sequence(best_seq, rng))

    starts.sort(key=first_key)

    starts = starts[:4]

    total_time = max(60.0, float(deadline - t0))
    work_dead = deadline - 240.0
    if work_dead < t0 + 120.0:
        work_dead = deadline - 30.0

    work_fracs = [0.52, 0.22, 0.15, 0.11]
    cum = 0.0
    for rank, item in enumerate(starts):
        if rank < len(work_fracs):
            cum += work_fracs[rank]
        else:
            cum += 0.05
        sub_dead = t0 + cum * (work_dead - t0)
        sub_dead = min(float(sub_dead), float(deadline - 10.0))
        if time.monotonic() > sub_dead - 30.0:
            continue
        sc, seq = refine_sequence(item[1], sub_dead, "start{}".format(rank + 1))
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc)
            best_seq = seq.copy()

    # Symmetrization can only be accepted if it is strictly better.
    if best_seq.size > 1:
        sym = 0.5 * (best_seq + best_seq[::-1])
        sym = normalize_sum(sym)
        sc_sym = exact_score(sym)
        if np.isfinite(sc_sym) and sc_sym < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc_sym)
            best_seq = sym

    # Final fixed high-beta LSE polish.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 70.0:
        ctx = make_fixed_lse_weight_ctx(best_seq, 30000.0, 0.0, 0.0)
        sc, seq = run_lbfgs_context(best_seq, ctx, deadline - 30.0, 4000)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc)
            best_seq = seq.copy()

    # A few controlled restarts if time remains.
    restarts = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 180.0 and restarts < 2:
        left = float(deadline - 180.0 - time.monotonic())
        if left < 90.0:
            break
        cand = perturb_sequence(best_seq, rng)
        sub_dead = time.monotonic() + min(left * 0.5, 320.0)
        sc, seq = refine_sequence(cand, sub_dead, "restart{}".format(restarts))
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc)
            best_seq = seq.copy()
        restarts += 1

    # Final multiplicative subgradient cleanup.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 15.0:
        sc, seq = subgrad_polish(best_seq, deadline - 5.0, max_iters=12)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc)
            best_seq = seq.copy()

    if best_seq.size > 1:
        sym = 0.5 * (best_seq + best_seq[::-1])
        sym = normalize_sum(sym)
        sc_sym = exact_score(sym)
        if np.isfinite(sc_sym) and sc_sym < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc_sym)
            best_seq = sym

    out = normalize_sum(best_seq)
    if out.size == 0:
        out = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    out = np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        out = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    elif s > 0:
        out = out / s
        out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)

    out_list = [float(x) for x in out]

    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out_list))
    except Exception:
        verified = float("nan")

    print("FINAL internal={:.8f} verified={:.8f} length={} time={:.1f}s".format(
        float(best_score), verified, len(out_list), time.monotonic() - t0), flush=True)

    return out_list
```
